- **RULES**: This file is the same in GATBOX, cabcrawl and ArcadeTech-Tracker. GATBOX owns it (`docs/agents/RULES.md`) and the other two vendor it byte-identical, with `CHECKSUMS` beside it and a test in every repository that also compares it with any sibling checkout. Change it only in GATBOX, then copy it to the other two in the same change. ([digitalunconciousness/cabcrawl](https://github.com/digitalunconciousness/cabcrawl/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RULES.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **rules**: Este proyecto usa [agent-context-suite](https://github.com/Gersom/agent-context-suite). No edites a mano `handoff.md`, `backlog.md` ni `history.md`: usa `acsuite task` (el detalle: `acsuite task --help`). El modo del plan se fija al empezar la tarea con `acsuite task start &lt;N&gt; --plan - --mode &lt;guided|step-by-step|auto|extreme&gt;` y se cambia con `acsuite task mode &lt;modo&gt;`. Las etiquetas de una tarea ([Gersom/agent-context-suite](https://github.com/Gersom/agent-context-suite/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/rules.md) · ★1)
- **rules**: - **Issue First, Then Branch.** GitHub Issues is the shared task list. Every issue has an assignee and labels, and work that crosses between collaborators is an issue before it starts. - **RUNBOOK**: 1. Read the rules: `ravi specs get cli/agents --mode rules --json`. 2. Reproduce the failing call with `--json` and read `error.code` first; the code, not the message, is the branch point. 3. Exit `1` + `AGENT_NOT_FOUND`: read `error.suggestions` — real agent ids and names similar to what was asked. Retry with one of them, or list with `ravi agents list --json`. 4. Exit `2`: read `error.acceptedFl ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cli/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · ★11)
- **RUNBOOK**: 1. `ravi agents list --json` — list agents visible to the current principal. 2. `ravi agents show &lt;id&gt; --json` — inspect one agent, including effective model fields (`effectiveProvider`, `effectiveModel`, `modelSource`, `modelPresetId`, `modelPresetVersion`). ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · ★11)
- **RUNBOOK**: - `state = active` with a `last_error`: the watcher could not finish the task or inform the session; it retries every 30 s. - `state = delivered`: the result reached the session and the Console acknowledgement is pending. - No login (`ravi whoami`): the watcher skips until `ravi login` again. ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cloud/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · ★11)
